Interpret finite-coefficient statements as limiting/extended-real shorthand,not arithmetic with an infinitely large REAL number. | 8227:TB:YM:C7:Teaching-figure:61 | graphic | A finite real endpoint limit is convergent. | 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:9 | text callout | Product,quotient,power rules use positive arguments and valid common basea>0,a≠1. The preceding prose unnecessarily saysa>1;the table itself and earlier reciprocal-base examples use the wider valid condition. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:10 | text callout | Write positive arguments as powers of the same valid base, multiply,then apply inverse log notation. Conditions travel with every step. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:11 | text callout | Use a quotient of positive powers and subtract exponents; denominator stays nonzero. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:12 | text callout | For positive original argument, raising to a real power multiplies its logarithm by that exponent. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:13 | text callout | log_a(1/x)=−log_a(x) forx>0 and a valid base; an outside minus is not a negative logarithm argument. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:14 | text callout | Distribute coefficientsoutside logs,not a sum/difference INSIDE an argument. Log of a product is a SUM,log quotient is a DIFFERENCE,and(loga)^n is notnlog(a). | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:15 | text callout | Keep argument positivity when splitting products,quotients and powers. Squared factors can admit original branches requiringlog absolute values. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:16 | text callout | Use the same base through all power,product and quotient steps. Original separate-log domains are retained after compression. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:17 | text callout | Rewrite numerical constants as logs of powers of the SAME base before combining. Forbase2,use3=log_2(8). | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:18 | text callout | Group only logs with equal bases;source exact groups evaluate2and−2. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:46 | text callout | Divide by5,takeln,and retain the original exponent2x+3before isolatingx. A rounded boundary is display metadata. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:47 | text callout | The original separate logs requirex,ypositive. Quotient(y+5)/y=x²leads to y5/(x²−1)onlyontheoriginalx>1branch. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:36 | text callout | Original affine exponent3−x and log(5/6)<0must both be retained. The final bound is not a log law for logA/logB. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:37 | text callout | e≈2.71828; e^x=y iff x=lny fory>0. This is the natural-definition objective,distinct from earlier arbitrary-base logs. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:38 | graphic | lne1,ln10,ln(e^x)=x ande^(lnx)=x withx>0where required. | same representation medium not recorded for linked demands in sampled NSSCAS parts | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:39 | text callout | The earlier common-base laws apply with basee and positive original arguments. No arbitrary change-of-base method is added. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:40 | text callout | Presenttime means t0 in the SUPPLIED model,not solving for a fitted growth rate. Units are millionpeopleandyears. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:41 | text callout | Calculator keys are procedure context;source mathematics is e^0.6 andln0.8with final3sf displays. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:42 | text callout | e^(lnx)=xneedsoriginalx>0. An outside sign/coefficient stays outside that inverse relation. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:43 | text callout | Take the ln of an isolated positive exponential,then solve its actual affine exponent. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:44 | text callout | Divide by7beforetakingln,andretain exponent coefficient3. Exactx=ln(320/7)/3. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:45 | text callout | Multiply by e^x>0toform the quadratic. Both2/3and4areadmissible;afterln the first gives a negative exponent,not an invalid root. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:1 | text callout | a^x=y iff log_a(y)=x. Preserve the base while exchanging exponent/output roles; real-domain conditions are required. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:2 | text callout | The source1.70 is an explicitly rounded3sf exponent for10^x50. Do not promote the displayed approximation to an exact identity. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:3 | text callout | The basea stays fixed; x andy exchange their index/log roles. The symbolic relation is not a request to approximate every numeric argument. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:4 | text callout | Base must bepositive and not1; argumentpositive. The0^0=0 line is crossed out as undefined,not endorsed. Negative exponents are admissible for valid positive bases. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:5 | text callout | An unlabelledlog here meansbase10. Exact internal values precede the requested final3sf displays; this is not the e/ln definition. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:6 | text callout | log_10(x)=1.2 meansx10^1.2; button layout is procedure context,not an additional assessed mathematical output. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:7 | text callout | log_a(a)=1,log_a(1)=0,log_a(a^x)=x and a^(log_a x)=x require basea>0,a≠1 andpositive argument where applicable. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
| 8227:TB:YM:C9:Teaching-figure:8 | text callout | Recognise integer/reciprocal/fractional powers to evaluate the source logs without a calculator. A helpful powers-up-to1000 suggestion does not extend syllabus scope. | not a graphic representation; internal text callout | none recorded |
